- **model-router**: Add custom models to Claude Code's /model picker. A local proxy routes each configured model to whatever backend you name - local vLLM, llama.cpp, Ollama, LM Studio, OpenRouter, a second Anthropic key - translating Anthropic&lt;-&gt;OpenAI in both directions including tools, images, reasoning and the SSE stream.
